   170→"acceptedAnswer": {
   171→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   172→"@type": "Answer",
   173→"text": " Yes. Windows 11 collects required diagnostic data to keep the system secure and functional. By default, it can also collect optional diagnostic data, app activity signals, search interactions, and personalization information. "
   174→}
   175→},
   176→{
   177→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   178→"@type": "Question",
   179→"name": "Can I completely disable telemetry in Windows 11?",
   180→"acceptedAnswer": {
   181→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   182→"@type": "Answer",
   183→"text": " No, not entirely. Required diagnostic data is built into the operating system. However, you can turn off optional diagnostic data, tailored experiences, feedback frequency, and activity tracking to minimize what is sent to Microsoft. "
   184→}
   185→},
   186→{
   187→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   188→"@type": "Question",
   189→"name": "Is switching to a local account better for privacy?",
   190→"acceptedAnswer": {
   191→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   192→"@type": "Answer",
   193→"text": " Yes. A local account reduces cloud synchronization of settings, preferences, app lists, and certain activity data. When you use a Microsoft account, Windows 11 connects more features to cloud services, including backup and cross-device sync. "
   194→}
   195→},
   196→{
   197→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   198→"@type": "Question",
   199→"name": "Does disabling Device Encryption make my PC less secure?",
   200→"acceptedAnswer": {
   201→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   202→"@type": "Answer",
   203→"text": " It can, if you do not replace it with another encryption method. Encryption protects your data if the device is lost or stolen. The privacy concern is that recovery keys may be stored in your Microsoft account. If you disable Device Encryption, you should use an alternative encryption solution to maintain data protection. "
   204→}
   205→},
   206→{
   207→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   208→"@type": "Question",
   209→"name": "What is Windows Recall, and should I disable it?",
   210→"acceptedAnswer": {
   211→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   212→"@type": "Answer",
   213→"text": " Windows Recall is an AI-powered feature that captures and indexes snapshots of your activity so you can search your past actions. While designed to run locally, it still records a timeline of on-device activity. If you are uncomfortable with continuous activity indexing, you can remove it from Windows Features. "
   214→}
   215→},
   216→{
   217→"@context": "https://schema.org",

   220→"acceptedAnswer": {
   221→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   222→"@type": "Answer",
   223→"text": " Yes, if you prefer to keep files strictly local. OneDrive continuously syncs files and some folders to the Microsoft cloud when signed in. Removing it stops automatic cloud synchronization. "
   224→}

   232→"@type": "Answer",
   233→"text": " No. Copilot is optional. It connects to Microsoft\u2019s cloud-based AI services. You can uninstall the Copilot app and disable Copilot integrations in Microsoft Edge if you prefer a more traditional desktop experience. "
   234→}
   235→},
   236→{
   237→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   238→"@type": "Question",

   240→"acceptedAnswer": {
   241→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   242→"@type": "Answer",
   243→"text": " It depends on your setup. If you use a local account, cloud backup of apps and preferences does not occur. If you use a Microsoft account, you can manually disable \u201cRemember my apps\u201d and \u201cRemember my preferences\u201d to prevent configuration data from syncing. "
   244→}

   246→{
   247→"@context": "https://schema.org",

   249→"name": "Should I disable Windows Update for privacy?",
   250→"acceptedAnswer": {
   251→"@context": "https://schema.org",
   252→"@type": "Answer",
   253→"text": " Completely disabling updates is not recommended for security reasons. Updates protect your device from vulnerabilities. If privacy is your concern, you can manually control updates or disable Delivery Optimization to prevent peer-to-peer update sharing. "
   254→}
